	% -----
	\subsubsection{Romans 6} % *ROMANS-6 
	% -----
		\label{ROMANS-6}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:1} % *ROMANS-6-1 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-1}

			WHAT shall we say then? Shall we continue in sin, that grace may abound?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τί οὖν ἐροῦμεν; ἐπιμένωμεν τῇ ἁμαρτίᾳ, ἵνα ἡ χάρις πλεονάσῃ;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:2} % *ROMANS-6-2 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-2}

			God forbid. How shall we, that are dead to sin, live any longer therein?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μὴ γένοιτο· οἵτινες ἀπεθάνομεν τῇ ἁμαρτίᾳ, πῶς ἔτι ζήσομεν ἐν αὐτῇ;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:3} % *ROMANS-6-3 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-3}

			Know ye not, that so many of us as were baptized into Jesus Christ were baptized into his death?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἢ ἀγνοεῖτε ὅτι ὅσοι ἐβαπτίσθημεν εἰς Χριστὸν Ἰησοῦν εἰς τὸν θάνατον αὐτοῦ ἐβαπτίσθημεν;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:4} % *ROMANS-6-4 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-4}

			Therefore we are buried with him by baptism into death: that like as Christ was raised up from the dead by the glory of the Father, even so we also should walk in newness of life.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{συνετάφημεν οὖν αὐτῷ διὰ τοῦ βαπτίσματος εἰς τὸν θάνατον, ἵνα ὥσπερ ἠγέρθη Χριστὸς ἐκ νεκρῶν διὰ τῆς δόξης τοῦ πατρός, οὕτως καὶ ἡμεῖς ἐν καινότητι ζωῆς περιπατήσωμεν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:5} % *ROMANS-6-5 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-5}

			For if we have been planted together in the likeness of his death, we shall be also in the likeness of his resurrection:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Εἰ γὰρ σύμφυτοι γεγόναμεν τῷ ὁμοιώματι τοῦ θανάτου αὐτοῦ, ἀλλὰ καὶ τῆς ἀναστάσεως ἐσόμεθα·}
					\item[AB-1993 429] For if we have grown into union with him through a death like his, we shall also be united with him through a resurrection like his.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:6} % *ROMANS-6-6 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-6}

			Knowing this, that our old man is crucified with him, that the body of sin might be destroyed, that henceforth we should not serve sin.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τοῦτο γινώσκοντες ὅτι ὁ παλαιὸς ἡμῶν ἄνθρωπος συνεσταυρώθη, ἵνα καταργηθῇ τὸ σῶμα τῆς ἁμαρτίας, τοῦ μηκέτι δουλεύειν ἡμᾶς τῇ ἁμαρτίᾳ,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:7} % *ROMANS-6-7 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-7}

			For he that is dead is freed from sin.%
			\footnote{%
				% \label{}%
				In context it seems that he's speaking of people who have been spiritually reborn, or are ``dead to sin'' through baptism or co-crucifixion with Christ, but I'm not sure. The next verse makes it seem like this at least, and the ones before.
				}

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁ γὰρ ἀποθανὼν δεδικαίωται ἀπὸ τῆς ἁμαρτίας.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:8} % *ROMANS-6-8 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-8}

			Now if we be dead with Christ, we believe that we shall also live with him:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ ἀπεθάνομεν σὺν Χριστῷ, πιστεύομεν ὅτι καὶ συζήσομεν αὐτῷ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:9} % *ROMANS-6-9 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-9}

			Knowing that Christ being raised from the dead dieth no more; death hath no more dominion over him.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰδότες ὅτι Χριστὸς ἐγερθεὶς ἐκ νεκρῶν οὐκέτι ἀποθνῄσκει, θάνατος αὐτοῦ οὐκέτι κυριεύει·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:10} % *ROMANS-6-10 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-10}

			For in that he died, he died unto sin once: but in that he liveth, he liveth unto God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὃ γὰρ ἀπέθανεν, τῇ ἁμαρτίᾳ ἀπέθανεν ἐφάπαξ· ὃ δὲ ζῇ, ζῇ τῷ θεῷ.}
					\item[BSV] quod enim mortuus est peccato mortuus est semel quod autem vivit vivit Deo
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					\item[ESV] For the death he died he died to sin, once for all, but the life he lives he lives to God.
					\item[NASB] For the death that He died, He died to sin once for all; but the life that He lives, He lives to God.
					\item[NIV] The death he died, he died to sin once for all; but the life he lives, he lives to God.
					\item[NRSV] The death he died, he died to sin, once for all; but the life he lives, he lives to God.
					\item[ASV] For the death that he died, he died unto sin once: but the life that he liveth, he liveth unto God.
					\item[NIRV] When he died, he died once and for all time as far as sin is concerned. Now that he lives, he lives as far as God is concerned.
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:11} % *ROMANS-6-11 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-11}

			Likewise reckon ye also yourselves%
				\footnote{%
					\label{ROMANS-6-11-reckon}%
					Connects back to all the uses of \gr{λογίζομαι} in \hyperref[ROMANS-4]{chapter 4}.
					}
			to be dead indeed unto sin, but alive unto God through Jesus Christ our Lord.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὕτως καὶ ὑμεῖς λογίζεσθε ἑαυτοὺς εἶναι νεκροὺς μὲν τῇ ἁμαρτίᾳ ζῶντας δὲ τῷ θεῷ ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:12} % *ROMANS-6-12 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-12}

			Let not sin therefore reign in your mortal body, that ye should obey it in the lusts thereof.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μὴ οὖν βασιλευέτω ἡ ἁμαρτία ἐν τῷ θνητῷ ὑμῶν σώματι εἰς τὸ ὑπακούειν ταῖς ἐπιθυμίαις αὐτοῦ,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:13} % *ROMANS-6-13 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-13}

			Neither yield ye your members as instruments of unrighteousness unto sin: but yield yourselves unto God, as those that are alive from the dead, and your members as instruments of righteousness unto God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μηδὲ παριστάνετε τὰ μέλη ὑμῶν ὅπλα ἀδικίας τῇ ἁμαρτίᾳ, ἀλλὰ παραστήσατε ἑαυτοὺς τῷ θεῷ ὡσεὶ ἐκ νεκρῶν ζῶντας καὶ τὰ μέλη ὑμῶν ὅπλα δικαιοσύνης τῷ θεῷ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:14} % *ROMANS-6-14 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-14}

			For sin shall not have dominion over you: for ye are not under the law, but under grace.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἁμαρτία γὰρ ὑμῶν οὐ κυριεύσει, οὐ γάρ ἐστε ὑπὸ νόμον ἀλλὰ ὑπὸ χάριν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:15} % *ROMANS-6-15 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-15}

			What then? shall we sin, because we are not under the law, but under grace? God forbid.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τί οὖν; ἁμαρτήσωμεν ὅτι οὐκ ἐσμὲν ὑπὸ νόμον ἀλλὰ ὑπὸ χάριν; μὴ γένοιτο·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:16} % *ROMANS-6-16 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-16}

			Know ye not, that to whom ye yield yourselves servants to obey, his servants ye are to whom ye obey; whether of sin unto death, or of obedience unto righteousness?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐκ οἴδατε ὅτι ᾧ παριστάνετε ἑαυτοὺς δούλους εἰς ὑπακοήν, δοῦλοί ἐστε ᾧ ὑπακούετε, ἤτοι ἁμαρτίας εἰς θάνατον ἢ ὑπακοῆς εἰς δικαιοσύνην;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:17} % *ROMANS-6-17 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-17}

			 But God be thanked, that ye were the servants of sin, but ye have obeyed from the heart that form of doctrine which was delivered you.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{χάρις δὲ τῷ θεῷ ὅτι ἦτε δοῦλοι τῆς ἁμαρτίας ὑπηκούσατε δὲ ἐκ καρδίας εἰς ὃν παρεδόθητε τύπον διδαχῆς,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:18} % *ROMANS-6-18 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-18}

			Being then made free from sin, ye became the servants of righteousness.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐλευθερωθέντες δὲ ἀπὸ τῆς ἁμαρτίας ἐδουλώθητε τῇ δικαιοσύνῃ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:19} % *ROMANS-6-19 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-19}

			I speak after the manner of men because of the infirmity of your flesh: for as ye have yielded your members servants to uncleanness and to iniquity unto iniquity; even so now yield your members servants to righteousness unto holiness.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀνθρώπινον λέγω διὰ τὴν ἀσθένειαν τῆς σαρκὸς ὑμῶν· ὥσπερ γὰρ παρεστήσατε τὰ μέλη ὑμῶν δοῦλα τῇ ἀκαθαρσίᾳ καὶ τῇ ἀνομίᾳ εἰς τὴν ἀνομίαν, οὕτως νῦν παραστήσατε τὰ μέλη ὑμῶν δοῦλα τῇ δικαιοσύνῃ εἰς ἁγιασμόν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:20} % *ROMANS-6-20 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-20}

			For when ye were the servants of sin, ye were free from righteousness.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὅτε γὰρ δοῦλοι ἦτε τῆς ἁμαρτίας, ἐλεύθεροι ἦτε τῇ δικαιοσύνῃ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:21} % *ROMANS-6-21 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-21}

			What fruit had ye then in those things whereof ye are now ashamed? for the end of those things is death.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τίνα οὖν καρπὸν εἴχετε τότε ἐφ᾽ οἷς νῦν ἐπαισχύνεσθε; τὸ γὰρ} \hyperref[TELOS]{\grl{τέλος}} \gr{ἐκείνων θάνατος·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:22} % *ROMANS-6-22 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-22}

			But now being made free from sin, and become servants to God, ye have your fruit unto holiness, and the end everlasting life.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νυνὶ δέ, ἐλευθερωθέντες ἀπὸ τῆς ἁμαρτίας δουλωθέντες δὲ τῷ θεῷ, ἔχετε τὸν καρπὸν ὑμῶν εἰς ἁγιασμόν, τὸ δὲ τέλος ζωὴν αἰώνιον.}
					\item[SPNT] But now that you have been freed freed from sin and have become God's slaves, your fruits are holy. That way ends in eternal life.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 6:23} % *ROMANS-6-23 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-6-23}

			For the wages of sin is death; but the gift of God is eternal life through Jesus Christ our Lord.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὰ γὰρ ὀψώνια τῆς ἁμαρτίας θάνατος, τὸ δὲ χάρισμα τοῦ θεοῦ ζωὴ αἰώνιος ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ τῷ κυρίῳ ἡμῶν.}
					\item[SPNT] Sin trades in death. God bestows eternal life, in our Lord Jesus the Messiah.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Romans 7} % *ROMANS-7 
	% -----
		\label{ROMANS-7}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:1} % *ROMANS-7-1 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-1}

			KNOW ye not, brethren, (for I speak to them that know the law,) how that the law hath dominion over a man as long as he liveth?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἢ ἀγνοεῖτε, ἀδελφοί, γινώσκουσιν γὰρ νόμον λαλῶ, ὅτι ὁ νόμος κυριεύει τοῦ ἀνθρώπου ἐφ᾽ ὅσον χρόνον ζῇ;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:2} % *ROMANS-7-2 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-2}

			For the woman which hath an husband is bound by the law to her husband so long as he liveth; but if the husband be dead, she is loosed from the law of her husband.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἡ γὰρ ὕπανδρος γυνὴ τῷ ζῶντι ἀνδρὶ δέδεται νόμῳ· ἐὰν δὲ ἀποθάνῃ ὁ ἀνήρ, κατήργηται ἀπὸ τοῦ νόμου τοῦ ἀνδρός.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:3} % *ROMANS-7-3 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-3}

			So then if, while her husband liveth, she be married to another man, she shall be called an adulteress: but if her husband be dead, she is free from that law; so that she is no adulteress, though she be married to another man.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἄρα οὖν ζῶντος τοῦ ἀνδρὸς μοιχαλὶς χρηματίσει ἐὰν γένηται ἀνδρὶ ἑτέρῳ· ἐὰν δὲ ἀποθάνῃ ὁ ἀνήρ, ἐλευθέρα ἐστὶν ἀπὸ τοῦ νόμου, τοῦ μὴ εἶναι αὐτὴν μοιχαλίδα γενομένην ἀνδρὶ ἑτέρῳ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:4} % *ROMANS-7-4 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-4}

			Wherefore, my brethren, ye also are become dead to the law by the body of Christ; that ye should be married to another, even to him who is raised from the dead, that we should bring forth fruit unto God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὥστε, ἀδελφοί μου, καὶ ὑμεῖς ἐθανατώθητε τῷ νόμῳ διὰ τοῦ σώματος τοῦ Χριστοῦ, εἰς τὸ γενέσθαι ὑμᾶς ἑτέρῳ, τῷ ἐκ νεκρῶν ἐγερθέντι ἵνα καρποφορήσωμεν τῷ θεῷ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:5} % *ROMANS-7-5 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-5}

			For when we were in the flesh, the motions of sins, which were by the law, did work in our members to bring forth fruit unto death.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτε γὰρ ἦμεν ἐν τῇ σαρκί, τὰ παθήματα τῶν ἁμαρτιῶν τὰ διὰ τοῦ νόμου ἐνηργεῖτο ἐν τοῖς μέλεσιν ἡμῶν εἰς τὸ καρποφορῆσαι τῷ θανάτῳ·}
					\item[SPNT] When we were fleshly, sin's diseases, which come through the Law, were active in the parts of our bodies, and yielded fruits for death.
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:6} % *ROMANS-7-6 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-6}

			But now we are delivered from the law, that being dead wherein we were held; that we should serve in newness of spirit, and not in the oldness of the letter.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νυνὶ δὲ κατηργήθημεν ἀπὸ τοῦ νόμου, ἀποθανόντες ἐν ᾧ κατειχόμεθα, ὥστε δουλεύειν ἡμᾶς ἐν καινότητι πνεύματος καὶ οὐ παλαιότητι γράμματος.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:7} % *ROMANS-7-7 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-7}

			What shall we say then? Is the law sin? God forbid. Nay, I had not known sin, but by the law: for I had not known lust, except the law had said,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Very weird translation to take this as ``covet,'' since it obscures the obvious relation between \gr{ἐπιθυμίαν} and \gr{ἐπιθυμήσεις}.
					}%
			Thou shalt not covet.%
				\footnote{%
					\label{ROMANS-7-7-known}%
					This last thought seems false---though maybe he means that he didn't know \textit{that} lust was a sin? He can't mean that he discovered lust through the law, I think.
					}

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τί οὖν ἐροῦμεν; ὁ νόμος ἁμαρτία; μὴ γένοιτο· ἀλλὰ τὴν ἁμαρτίαν οὐκ ἔγνων εἰ μὴ διὰ νόμου, τήν τε γὰρ ἐπιθυμίαν οὐκ ᾔδειν εἰ μὴ ὁ νόμος ἔλεγεν· Οὐκ ἐπιθυμήσεις·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:8} % *ROMANS-7-8 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-8}

			But sin, taking occasion by the commandment, wrought in me all manner of concupiscence. For without the law sin was dead.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀφορμὴν δὲ λαβοῦσα ἡ ἁμαρτία διὰ τῆς ἐντολῆς κατειργάσατο ἐν ἐμοὶ πᾶσαν ἐπιθυμίαν, χωρὶς γὰρ νόμου ἁμαρτία νεκρά.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:9} % *ROMANS-7-9 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-9}

			For I was alive without the law once: but when the commandment came, sin revived, and I died.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ δὲ ἔζων χωρὶς νόμου ποτέ· ἐλθούσης δὲ τῆς ἐντολῆς ἡ ἁμαρτία ἀνέζησεν,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:10} % *ROMANS-7-10 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-10}

			And the commandment, which was ordained to life, I found to be unto death.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐγὼ δὲ ἀπέθανον, καὶ εὑρέθη μοι ἡ ἐντολὴ ἡ εἰς ζωὴν αὕτη εἰς θάνατον·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:11} % *ROMANS-7-11 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-11}

			For sin, taking occasion by the commandment, deceived me, and by it slew me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἡ γὰρ ἁμαρτία ἀφορμὴν λαβοῦσα διὰ τῆς ἐντολῆς ἐξηπάτησέν με καὶ δι᾽ αὐτῆς ἀπέκτεινεν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:12} % *ROMANS-7-12 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-12}

			Wherefore the law is holy, and the commandment holy,
				\footnote{%
				% \label{ROMANS-7-12-just}%
				Interesting combination of words here at the end; see \hyperref[MORONI-10-6]{Moroni 10:6}.
				}%
			and just, and good.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὥστε ὁ μὲν νόμος ἅγιος, καὶ ἡ ἐντολὴ ἁγία καὶ δικαία καὶ ἀγαθή.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:13} % *ROMANS-7-13 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-13}

			Was then that which is good made death unto me? God forbid. But sin, that it might appear sin, working death in me by that which is good; that sin by the commandment might become exceeding sinful.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τὸ οὖν ἀγαθὸν ἐμοὶ ἐγένετο θάνατος; μὴ γένοιτο· ἀλλὰ ἡ ἁμαρτία, ἵνα φανῇ ἁμαρτία διὰ τοῦ ἀγαθοῦ μοι κατεργαζομένη θάνατον· ἵνα γένηται καθ᾽ ὑπερβολὴν ἁμαρτωλὸς ἡ ἁμαρτία διὰ τῆς ἐντολῆς.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:14} % *ROMANS-7-14 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-14}

			For we know that the law is spiritual: but I am carnal, sold under sin.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οἴδαμεν γὰρ ὅτι ὁ νόμος πνευματικός ἐστιν· ἐγὼ δὲ σάρκινός εἰμι, πεπραμένος ὑπὸ τὴν ἁμαρτίαν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:15} % *ROMANS-7-15 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-15}

			For that which I do I allow not: for what I would, that do I not; but what I hate, that do I.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὃ γὰρ κατεργάζομαι οὐ γινώσκω· οὐ γὰρ ὃ θέλω τοῦτο πράσσω, ἀλλ᾽ ὃ μισῶ τοῦτο ποιῶ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:16} % *ROMANS-7-16 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-16}

			If then I do that which I would not, I consent unto the law that it is good.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ ὃ οὐ θέλω τοῦτο ποιῶ, σύμφημι τῷ νόμῳ ὅτι καλός.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:17} % *ROMANS-7-17 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-17}

			Now then it is no more I that do it, but sin that dwelleth in me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{νυνὶ δὲ οὐκέτι ἐγὼ κατεργάζομαι αὐτὸ ἀλλὰ ἡ οἰκοῦσα ἐν ἐμοὶ ἁμαρτία.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:18} % *ROMANS-7-18 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-18}

			For I know that in me (that is, in my flesh,) dwelleth no good thing: for to will is present with me; but how to perform that which is good I find not.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οἶδα γὰρ ὅτι οὐκ οἰκεῖ ἐν ἐμοί, τοῦτ᾽ ἔστιν ἐν τῇ σαρκί μου, ἀγαθόν· τὸ γὰρ θέλειν παράκειταί μοι, τὸ δὲ κατεργάζεσθαι τὸ καλὸν οὔ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:19} % *ROMANS-7-19 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-19}

			For the good that I would I do not: but the evil which I would not, that I do.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ γὰρ ὃ θέλω ποιῶ ἀγαθόν, ἀλλὰ ὃ οὐ θέλω κακὸν τοῦτο πράσσω.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:20} % *ROMANS-7-20 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-20}

			Now if I do that I would not, it is no more I that do it, but sin that dwelleth in me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ ὃ οὐ θέλω τοῦτο ποιῶ, οὐκέτι ἐγὼ κατεργάζομαι αὐτὸ ἀλλὰ ἡ οἰκοῦσα ἐν ἐμοὶ ἁμαρτία.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:21} % *ROMANS-7-21 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-21}

			I find then a law, that, when I would do good, evil is present with me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Εὑρίσκω ἄρα τὸν νόμον τῷ θέλοντι ἐμοὶ ποιεῖν τὸ καλὸν ὅτι ἐμοὶ τὸ κακὸν παράκειται·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:22} % *ROMANS-7-22 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-22}

			For I delight in the law of God after the inward man:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{συνήδομαι γὰρ τῷ νόμῳ τοῦ θεοῦ κατὰ τὸν ἔσω ἄνθρωπον,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:23} % *ROMANS-7-23 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-23}

			But I see another law in my members, warring against the law of my mind, and bringing me into captivity to the law of sin which is in my members.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{βλέπω δὲ ἕτερον νόμον ἐν τοῖς μέλεσίν μου ἀντιστρατευόμενον τῷ νόμῳ τοῦ νοός μου καὶ αἰχμαλωτίζοντά με ἐν τῷ νόμῳ τῆς ἁμαρτίας τῷ ὄντι ἐν τοῖς μέλεσίν μου.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:24} % *ROMANS-7-24 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-24}

			O wretched man that I am! who shall deliver me from the body of this death?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ταλαίπωρος ἐγὼ ἄνθρωπος· τίς με ῥύσεται ἐκ τοῦ σώματος τοῦ θανάτου τούτου;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 7:25} % *ROMANS-7-25 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-7-25}

			I thank God through Jesus Christ our Lord. So then with the mind I myself serve the law of God; but with the flesh the law of sin.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{χάρις τῷ θεῷ διὰ Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ τοῦ κυρίου ἡμῶν. Ἄρα οὖν αὐτὸς ἐγὼ τῷ μὲν νοῒ δουλεύω νόμῳ θεοῦ, τῇ δὲ σαρκὶ νόμῳ ἁμαρτίας.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Romans 8} % *ROMANS-8 
	% -----
		\label{ROMANS-8}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:1} % *ROMANS-8-1 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-1}

			THERE is therefore now no condemnation to them which are in Christ Jesus,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The rest of this verse isn't in SBLG.
					}%
			who walk not after the flesh, but after the Spirit.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὐδὲν ἄρα νῦν κατάκριμα τοῖς ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ·}
					% \item[AB ]

					\item[SBLG] \gr{οἱ γὰρ κατὰ σάρκα ὄντες τὰ τῆς σαρκὸς φρονοῦσιν, οἱ δὲ κατὰ πνεῦμα τὰ τοῦ πνεύματος.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:6} % *ROMANS-8-6 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-6}

			For to be carnally minded is death; but to be spiritually minded is life and peace.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τὸ γὰρ φρόνημα}\footnotemark \gr{τῆς σαρκὸς θάνατος, τὸ δὲ φρόνημα τοῦ πνεύματος ζωὴ καὶ εἰρήνη·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}
			\footnotetext{%
				\label{PHRONEMA}%
				\gr{φρόνημα} occurs four times in the Greek NT, and all are in this chapter: 2 times in this verse, 1 time in \hyperref[ROMANS-8-7]{verse 7}, and 1 time in \hyperref[ROMANS-8-27]{verse 27}. (Note the use of the related \gr{φρονέω} in the previous verse.) For \gr{φρόνημα} BDAG 948b reads: ``\textbf{the faculty of fixing one's mind on something, \textit{way of thinking, mind(-set)}}, in our literature (only Romans 8) with focus on strong intention \textit{aim, aspiration, striving}.'' CGL 1479a:
					\begin{compactitem}
						\item[1] \textbf{mind} (of a person or god); \textbf{mentality, state of mind, outlook} (of a person); \textbf{attitude} (towards someone)
						\item[2] \textbf{will} (of a god, a person); plural, intentions; ambitions (usually with pejorative connotation)
						\item[3] \textbf{spirit, confidence} (of a person or nation); \textbf{pride} (as a positive trait)
						\item[4] \textbf{arrogance}
					\end{compactitem}
				See also TDNT 9:232: ``\gr{φρόνημα} occurs in the NT only in connection with \gr{φρονέω} (Romans 8:5) and it means the same as the infinitive \gr{τὸ φρονεῖν} used as a noun. The \gr{τὰ ἐπίγεια φρονοῦντες} (\hyperref[PHILIPPIANS-3-19]{Philippians 3:19}, cf. \gr{ἐν σαρκὶ πεποιθότες} (\hyperref[PHILIPPIANS-3-3]{3:3}) are entangled in a superstition and their thinking is governed by earthly powers even though they belong to the Christian community. Paul presses toward the mark, his upward calling by God in Christ Jesus, as the prize of victory, \hyperref[PHILIPPIANS-3-14]{Philippians 3:14}. This sayin about himself gives the \gr{τέλειοι} in the congregation their basic orientation. With the indicative \gr{φρονοῦμεν} we also find the cohortative \gr{φρονῶμεν} as a variant reading at \hyperref[PHILIPPIANS-3-15]{Philippians 3:15}; since the orientation is finally a gift of God's Spirit, the variant is of no material significance. With \gr{τὰ ἄνω ζητεῖτε} and \gr{τὰ ἄνω φρονεῖτε} (\hyperref[COLOSSIANS-3-1]{Colossians 3:1 f.}) we have the Pauline imperative, obeying which is not left to the will of man, for the basis here is the uniting of the believer in baptism with the Christ event and its eschatological reference. \gr{τὸ φρόνημα τοῦ πνεύματος}, the gift which determines man, is finally God Himself, \hyperref[ROMANS-8-27]{Romans 8:27}.''
				}

		\paragraph{Romans 8:23} % *ROMANS-8-23 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-23}

			And not only they, but ourselves also, which have the firstfruits of the Spirit, even we ourselves groan within ourselves, waiting for the adoption, to wit, the redemption of our body.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ μόνον δέ, ἀλλὰ καὶ αὐτοὶ τὴν ἀπαρχὴν τοῦ πνεύματος ἔχοντες ἡμεῖς καὶ αὐτοὶ ἐν ἑαυτοῖς στενάζομεν, υἱοθεσίαν ἀπεκδεχόμενοι τὴν ἀπολύτρωσιν τοῦ σώματος ἡμῶν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:24} % *ROMANS-8-24 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-24}

			For we are saved by hope: but hope that is seen is not hope: for what a man seeth, why doth he yet hope for?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τῇ γὰρ ἐλπίδι ἐσώθημεν· ἐλπὶς δὲ βλεπομένη οὐκ ἔστιν ἐλπίς, ὃ γὰρ βλέπει τίς ἐλπίζει;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:25} % *ROMANS-8-25 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-25}

			But if we hope for that we see not, then do we with patience wait for it.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ ὃ οὐ βλέπομεν ἐλπίζομεν, δι᾽ ὑπομονῆς ἀπεκδεχόμεθα.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:26} % *ROMANS-8-26 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-26}

			Likewise the Spirit also helpeth our infirmities: for we know not what we should pray for as we ought: but the Spirit itself maketh intercession for us with groanings which cannot be uttered.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὡσαύτως δὲ καὶ τὸ πνεῦμα συναντιλαμβάνεται τῇ ἀσθενείᾳ ἡμῶν· τὸ γὰρ τί προσευξώμεθα καθὸ δεῖ οὐκ οἴδαμεν, ἀλλὰ αὐτὸ τὸ πνεῦμα ὑπερεντυγχάνει στεναγμοῖς ἀλαλήτοις,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:27} % *ROMANS-8-27 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-27}

			And he that searcheth%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Reminds me of \hyperref[1CORINTHIANS-2-10]{1 Corinthians 2:10}.
					}
			the hearts knoweth what is the mind of the Spirit, because he maketh intercession for the saints according to the will of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὁ δὲ} \hyperref[ERAUNAO]{\grl{ἐραυνῶν}} \gr{τὰς καρδίας οἶδεν τί τὸ φρόνημα τοῦ πνεύματος, ὅτι κατὰ θεὸν ἐντυγχάνει ὑπὲρ ἁγίων.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:28} % *ROMANS-8-28 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-28}

			And we know that all things work together for good to them that love God, to them who are the called according to his purpose.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οἴδαμεν δὲ ὅτι τοῖς ἀγαπῶσι τὸν θεὸν πάντα συνεργεῖ εἰς ἀγαθόν, τοῖς κατὰ πρόθεσιν κλητοῖς οὖσιν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:29} % *ROMANS-8-29 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-29}

			For whom he did foreknow, he also did predestinate to be conformed to the image of his Son, that he might be the firstborn among many brethren.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι οὓς προέγνω, καὶ προώρισεν συμμόρφους τῆς εἰκόνος τοῦ υἱοῦ αὐτοῦ, εἰς τὸ εἶναι αὐτὸν πρωτότοκον ἐν πολλοῖς ἀδελφοῖς·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:30} % *ROMANS-8-30 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-30}

			Moreover whom he did predestinate, them he also called: and whom he called, them he also justified: and whom he justified, them he also glorified.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὓς δὲ προώρισεν, τούτους καὶ ἐκάλεσεν· καὶ οὓς ἐκάλεσεν, τούτους καὶ ἐδικαίωσεν· οὓς δὲ ἐδικαίωσεν, τούτους καὶ ἐδόξασεν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:31} % *ROMANS-8-31 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-31}

			What shall we then say to these things? If God be for us, who can be against us?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τί οὖν ἐροῦμεν πρὸς ταῦτα; εἰ ὁ θεὸς ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν, τίς καθ᾽ ἡμῶν;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:32} % *ROMANS-8-32 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-32}

			He that spared not his own Son, but delivered him up for us all, how shall he not with him also freely give us all things?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅς γε τοῦ ἰδίου υἱοῦ οὐκ ἐφείσατο, ἀλλὰ ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν πάντων παρέδωκεν αὐτόν, πῶς οὐχὶ καὶ σὺν αὐτῷ τὰ πάντα ἡμῖν χαρίσεται;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:33} % *ROMANS-8-33 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-33}

			Who shall lay any thing to the charge of God's elect? It is God that justifieth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τίς ἐγκαλέσει κατὰ ἐκλεκτῶν θεοῦ; θεὸς ὁ δικαιῶν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:34} % *ROMANS-8-34 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-34}

			Who is he that condemneth? It is Christ that died, yea rather, that is risen again, who is even at the right hand of God, who also maketh intercession for us.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τίς ὁ κατακρινῶν; Χριστὸς ὁ ἀποθανών, μᾶλλον δὲ ἐγερθείς, ὅς καί ἐστιν ἐν δεξιᾷ τοῦ θεοῦ, ὃς καὶ ἐντυγχάνει ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:35} % *ROMANS-8-35 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-35}

			Who shall separate us from the love of Christ? shall tribulation, or distress, or persecution, or famine, or nakedness, or peril, or sword?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τίς ἡμᾶς χωρίσει ἀπὸ τῆς ἀγάπης τοῦ Χριστοῦ; θλῖψις ἢ στενοχωρία ἢ διωγμὸς ἢ λιμὸς ἢ γυμνότης ἢ κίνδυνος ἢ μάχαιρα;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:36} % *ROMANS-8-36 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-36}

			As it is written, For thy sake we are killed all the day long; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καθὼς γέγραπται ὅτι Ἕνεκεν σοῦ θανατούμεθα ὅλην τὴν ἡμέραν, ἐλογίσθημεν ὡς πρόβατα σφαγῆς.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:37} % *ROMANS-8-37 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-37}

			Nay, in all these things we are more than conquerors through him that loved us.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ ἐν τούτοις πᾶσιν ὑπερνικῶμεν διὰ τοῦ ἀγαπήσαντος ἡμᾶς.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:38} % *ROMANS-8-38 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-38}

			For I am persuaded, that neither death, nor life, nor angels, nor principalities, nor powers, nor things present, nor things to come,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πέπεισμαι γὰρ ὅτι οὔτε θάνατος οὔτε ζωὴ οὔτε ἄγγελοι οὔτε ἀρχαὶ οὔτε ἐνεστῶτα οὔτε μέλλοντα οὔτε δυνάμεις}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 8:39} % *ROMANS-8-39 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-8-39}

			Nor height, nor depth, nor any other creature, shall be able to separate us from the love of God, which is in Christ Jesus our Lord.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὔτε ὕψωμα οὔτε βάθος οὔτε τις κτίσις ἑτέρα δυνήσεται ἡμᾶς χωρίσαι ἀπὸ τῆς ἀγάπης τοῦ θεοῦ τῆς ἐν Χριστῷ Ἰησοῦ τῷ κυρίῳ ἡμῶν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Romans 9} % *ROMANS-9 
	% -----
		\label{ROMANS-9}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:1} % *ROMANS-9-1 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-1}

			I SAY the truth in Christ, I lie not, my conscience also bearing me witness in the Holy Ghost,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἀλήθειαν λέγω ἐν Χριστῷ, οὐ ψεύδομαι, συμμαρτυρούσης μοι τῆς συνειδήσεώς μου ἐν πνεύματι ἁγίῳ,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:2} % *ROMANS-9-2 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-2}

			That I have great heaviness and continual sorrow in my heart.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι λύπη μοί ἐστιν μεγάλη καὶ ἀδιάλειπτος ὀδύνη τῇ καρδίᾳ μου·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:3} % *ROMANS-9-3 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-3}

			For I could wish that myself were accursed from Christ for my brethren, my kinsmen according to the flesh:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ηὐχόμην γὰρ ἀνάθεμα εἶναι αὐτὸς ἐγὼ ἀπὸ τοῦ Χριστοῦ ὑπὲρ τῶν ἀδελφῶν μου τῶν συγγενῶν μου κατὰ σάρκα,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:4} % *ROMANS-9-4 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-4}

			Who are Israelites; to whom pertaineth the adoption, and the glory, and the covenants, and the giving of the law, and the service of God, and the promises;

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οἵτινές εἰσιν Ἰσραηλῖται, ὧν ἡ υἱοθεσία καὶ ἡ δόξα καὶ αἱ διαθῆκαι καὶ ἡ νομοθεσία καὶ ἡ λατρεία καὶ αἱ ἐπαγγελίαι,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:5} % *ROMANS-9-5 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-5}

			Whose are the fathers, and of whom as concerning the flesh Christ came, who is over all, God blessed for ever. Amen.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὧν οἱ πατέρες, καὶ ἐξ ὧν ὁ χριστὸς τὸ κατὰ σάρκα, ὁ ὢν ἐπὶ πάντων, θεὸς εὐλογητὸς εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας· ἀμήν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:6} % *ROMANS-9-6 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-6}

			Not as though the word of God hath taken none effect. For they are not all Israel, which are of Israel:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὐχ οἷον δὲ ὅτι ἐκπέπτωκεν ὁ λόγος τοῦ θεοῦ. οὐ γὰρ πάντες οἱ ἐξ Ἰσραήλ, οὗτοι Ἰσραήλ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:7} % *ROMANS-9-7 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-7}

			Neither, because they are the seed of Abraham, are they all children: but, In Isaac shall thy seed be called.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐδ᾽ ὅτι εἰσὶν σπέρμα Ἀβραάμ, πάντες τέκνα, ἀλλ᾽· Ἐν Ἰσαὰκ κληθήσεταί σοι σπέρμα.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 9:8} % *ROMANS-9-8 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-8}

			That is, They which are the children of the flesh, these are not the children of God: but the children of the promise are counted for the seed.

			\begin{framed}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγει γὰρ ἡ γραφὴ τῷ Φαραὼ ὅτι Εἰς αὐτὸ τοῦτο ἐξήγειρά σε ὅπως ἐνδείξωμαι ἐν σοὶ τὴν δύναμίν μου, καὶ ὅπως διαγγελῇ τὸ ὄνομά μου ἐν πάσῃ τῇ γῇ.}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ ἔσται ἐν τῷ τόπῳ οὗ ἐρρέθη αὐτοῖς· Οὐ λαός μου ὑμεῖς, ἐκεῖ κληθήσονται υἱοὶ θεοῦ ζῶντος.}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τί οὖν ἐροῦμεν; ὅτι ἔθνη τὰ μὴ διώκοντα δικαιοσύνην κατέλαβεν δικαιοσύνην, δικαιοσύνην δὲ τὴν ἐκ πίστεως·}

					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἰσραὴλ δὲ διώκων νόμον δικαιοσύνης εἰς νόμον οὐκ ἔφθασεν.}

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{διὰ τί; ὅτι οὐκ ἐκ πίστεως ἀλλ᾽ ὡς ἐξ ἔργων· προσέκοψαν τῷ λίθῳ τοῦ προσκόμματος,}

		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-9-33}

			As it is written, Behold, I lay in Sion a stumblingstone and rock of offence: and whosoever believeth on him shall not be ashamed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καθὼς γέγραπται· Ἰδοὺ τίθημι ἐν Σιὼν λίθον προσκόμματος καὶ πέτραν σκανδάλου, καὶ ὁ πιστεύων ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ οὐ καταισχυνθήσεται.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Romans 10} % *ROMANS-10 
	% -----
		\label{ROMANS-10}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:1} % *ROMANS-10-1 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-1}

			BRETHREN, my heart's desire and prayer to God for Israel is, that they might be saved.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἀδελφοί, ἡ μὲν εὐδοκία τῆς ἐμῆς καρδίας καὶ ἡ δέησις πρὸς τὸν θεὸν ὑπὲρ αὐτῶν εἰς σωτηρίαν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:2} % *ROMANS-10-2 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-2}

			For I bear them record that they have a zeal of God, but not according to knowledge.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μαρτυρῶ γὰρ αὐτοῖς ὅτι ζῆλον θεοῦ ἔχουσιν· ἀλλ᾽ οὐ κατ᾽ ἐπίγνωσιν,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:3} % *ROMANS-10-3 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-3}

			For they being ignorant of God's righteousness, and going about to establish their own righteousness, have not submitted themselves unto the righteousness of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀγνοοῦντες γὰρ τὴν τοῦ θεοῦ δικαιοσύνην, καὶ τὴν ἰδίαν ζητοῦντες στῆσαι, τῇ δικαιοσύνῃ τοῦ θεοῦ οὐχ ὑπετάγησαν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:4} % *ROMANS-10-4 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-4}

			For Christ is the end of the law for righteousness to every one that believeth.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{τέλος γὰρ νόμου Χριστὸς εἰς δικαιοσύνην παντὶ τῷ πιστεύοντι.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:5} % *ROMANS-10-5 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-5}

			For Moses describeth the righteousness which is of the law, That the man which doeth those things shall live by them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Μωϋσῆς γὰρ γράφει ὅτι τὴν δικαιοσύνην τὴν ἐκ τοῦ νόμου ὁ ποιήσας ἄνθρωπος ζήσεται ἐν αὐτῇ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:6} % *ROMANS-10-6 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-6}

			But the righteousness which is of faith speaketh on this wise, Say not in thine heart, Who shall ascend into heaven? (that is, to bring Christ down from above:)

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἡ δὲ ἐκ πίστεως δικαιοσύνη οὕτως λέγει· Μὴ εἴπῃς ἐν τῇ καρδίᾳ σου· Τίς ἀναβήσεται εἰς τὸν οὐρανόν; τοῦτ᾽ ἔστιν Χριστὸν καταγαγεῖν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]

				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:7} % *ROMANS-10-7 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-7}

			Or, Who shall descend into the deep? (that is, to bring up Christ again from the dead.)

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἤ· Τίς καταβήσεται εἰς τὴν ἄβυσσον; τοῦτ᾽ ἔστιν Χριστὸν ἐκ νεκρῶν ἀναγαγεῖν.}
					% \item[AB ]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:8} % *ROMANS-10-8 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-8}

			But what saith it? The word is nigh thee, even in thy mouth, and in thy heart: that is, the word of faith, which we preach;

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλὰ τί λέγει; Ἐγγύς σου τὸ ῥῆμά ἐστιν, ἐν τῷ στόματί σου καὶ ἐν τῇ καρδίᾳ σου, τοῦτ᾽ ἔστιν τὸ ῥῆμα τῆς πίστεως ὃ κηρύσσομεν.}

					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:9} % *ROMANS-10-9 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-9}

			That if thou shalt confess with thy mouth the Lord Jesus, and shalt believe in thine heart that God hath raised him from the dead, thou shalt be saved.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι ἐὰν ὁμολογήσῃς ἐν τῷ στόματί σου κύριον Ἰησοῦν, καὶ πιστεύσῃς ἐν τῇ καρδίᾳ σου ὅτι ὁ θεὸς αὐτὸν ἤγειρεν ἐκ νεκρῶν, σωθήσῃ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:10} % *ROMANS-10-10 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-10}

			For with the heart man believeth unto righteousness; and with the mouth confession is made unto salvation.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καρδίᾳ γὰρ πιστεύεται εἰς δικαιοσύνην, στόματι δὲ ὁμολογεῖται εἰς σωτηρίαν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:11} % *ROMANS-10-11 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-11}

			For the scripture saith, Whosoever believeth on him shall not be ashamed.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{λέγει γὰρ ἡ γραφή· Πᾶς ὁ πιστεύων ἐπ᾽ αὐτῷ οὐ καταισχυνθήσεται.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:12} % *ROMANS-10-12 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-12}

			For there is no difference between the Jew and the Greek: for the same Lord over all is rich unto all that call upon him.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐ γάρ ἐστιν διαστολὴ Ἰουδαίου τε καὶ Ἕλληνος, ὁ γὰρ αὐτὸς κύριος πάντων, πλουτῶν εἰς πάντας τοὺς ἐπικαλουμένους αὐτόν·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:13} % *ROMANS-10-13 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-13}

			For whosoever shall call upon the name of the Lord shall be saved.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Πᾶς γὰρ ὃς ἂν ἐπικαλέσηται τὸ ὄνομα κυρίου σωθήσεται.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:14} % *ROMANS-10-14 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-14}

			How then shall they call on him in whom they have not believed? and how shall they believe in him of whom they have not heard? and how shall they hear without a preacher?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Πῶς οὖν ἐπικαλέσωνται εἰς ὃν οὐκ ἐπίστευσαν; πῶς δὲ πιστεύσωσιν οὗ οὐκ ἤκουσαν; πῶς δὲ ἀκούσωσιν χωρὶς κηρύσσοντος;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:15} % *ROMANS-10-15 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-15}

			And how shall they preach, except they be sent? as it is written, How beautiful are the feet of them that preach the gospel of peace, and bring glad tidings of good things!

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πῶς δὲ κηρύξωσιν ἐὰν μὴ ἀποσταλῶσιν; καθὼς γέγραπται· Ὡς ὡραῖοι οἱ πόδες τῶν εὐαγγελιζομένων τὰ ἀγαθά.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:16} % *ROMANS-10-16 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-16}

			But they have not all obeyed the gospel. For Esaias saith, Lord, who hath believed our report?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλ᾽ οὐ πάντες ὑπήκουσαν τῷ εὐαγγελίῳ· Ἠσαΐας γὰρ λέγει· Κύριε, τίς ἐπίστευσεν τῇ ἀκοῇ ἡμῶν;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:17} % *ROMANS-10-17 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-17}

			So then faith cometh by hearing, and hearing by the word of God.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἄρα ἡ πίστις ἐξ ἀκοῆς, ἡ δὲ ἀκοὴ διὰ ῥήματος Χριστοῦ.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:18} % *ROMANS-10-18 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-18}

			But I say, Have they not heard? Yes verily, their sound went into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἀλλὰ λέγω, μὴ οὐκ ἤκουσαν; μενοῦνγε· Εἰς πᾶσαν τὴν γῆν ἐξῆλθεν ὁ φθόγγος αὐτῶν, καὶ εἰς τὰ πέρατα τῆς οἰκουμένης τὰ ῥήματα αὐτῶν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:19} % *ROMANS-10-19 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-19}

			But I say, Did not Israel know? First Moses saith, I will provoke you to jealousy by them that are no people, and by a foolish nation I will anger you.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀλλὰ λέγω, μὴ Ἰσραὴλ οὐκ ἔγνω; πρῶτος Μωϋσῆς λέγει· Ἐγὼ παραζηλώσω ὑμᾶς ἐπ᾽ οὐκ ἔθνει, ἐπ᾽ ἔθνει ἀσυνέτῳ παροργιῶ ὑμᾶς.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:20} % *ROMANS-10-20 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-20}

			But Esaias is very bold, and saith, I was found of them that sought me not; I was made manifest unto them that asked not after me.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ἠσαΐας δὲ ἀποτολμᾷ καὶ λέγει· Εὑρέθην ἐν τοῖς ἐμὲ μὴ ζητοῦσιν, ἐμφανὴς ἐγενόμην τοῖς ἐμὲ μὴ ἐπερωτῶσιν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 10:21} % *ROMANS-10-21 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-10-21}

			But to Israel he saith, All day long I have stretched forth my hands unto a disobedient and gainsaying people.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{πρὸς δὲ τὸν Ἰσραὴλ λέγει· Ὅλην τὴν ἡμέραν ἐξεπέτασα τὰς χεῖράς μου πρὸς λαὸν ἀπειθοῦντα καὶ ἀντιλέγοντα.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Romans 11} % *ROMANS-11 
	% -----
		\label{ROMANS-11}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:1} % *ROMANS-11-1 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-1}

			I SAY then, Hath God cast away his people? God forbid. For I also am an Israelite, of the seed of Abraham, of the tribe of Benjamin.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Λέγω οὖν, μὴ ἀπώσατο ὁ θεὸς τὸν λαὸν αὐτοῦ; μὴ γένοιτο· καὶ γὰρ ἐγὼ Ἰσραηλίτης εἰμί, ἐκ σπέρματος Ἀβραάμ, φυλῆς Βενιαμίν.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:2} % *ROMANS-11-2 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-2}

			God hath not cast away his people which he foreknew. Wot ye not what the scripture saith of Elias? how he maketh intercession to God against Israel, saying,

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὐκ ἀπώσατο ὁ θεὸς τὸν λαὸν αὐτοῦ ὃν προέγνω. ἢ οὐκ οἴδατε ἐν Ἠλίᾳ τί λέγει ἡ γραφή, ὡς ἐντυγχάνει τῷ θεῷ κατὰ τοῦ Ἰσραήλ;}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:3} % *ROMANS-11-3 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-3}

		% ---

			And if by grace, then is it no more of works: otherwise grace is no more grace.
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The rest of the verse is not in SBLG.
					}%

			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ χάριτι, οὐκέτι ἐξ ἔργων, ἐπεὶ ἡ χάρις οὐκέτι γίνεται χάρις.}

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{σκοτισθήτωσαν οἱ ὀφθαλμοὶ αὐτῶν τοῦ μὴ βλέπειν, καὶ τὸν νῶτον αὐτῶν διὰ παντὸς σύγκαμψον.}

		\paragraph{Romans 11:11} % *ROMANS-11-11 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-11}

			I say then, Have they stumbled that they should fall? God forbid: but rather through their fall salvation is come unto the Gentiles, for to provoke them to jealousy.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Λέγω οὖν, μὴ ἔπταισαν ἵνα πέσωσιν; μὴ γένοιτο· ἀλλὰ τῷ αὐτῶν παραπτώματι ἡ σωτηρία τοῖς ἔθνεσιν, εἰς τὸ παραζηλῶσαι αὐτούς.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:12} % *ROMANS-11-12 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-12}

			Now if the fall of them be the riches of the world, and the diminishing of them the riches of the Gentiles; how much more their fulness?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ τὸ παράπτωμα αὐτῶν πλοῦτος κόσμου καὶ τὸ ἥττημα αὐτῶν πλοῦτος ἐθνῶν, πόσῳ μᾶλλον τὸ πλήρωμα αὐτῶν.}

		% ---

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὑμῖν δὲ λέγω τοῖς ἔθνεσιν. ἐφ᾽ ὅσον μὲν οὖν εἰμι ἐγὼ ἐθνῶν ἀπόστολος, τὴν διακονίαν μου} \hyperref[DOXAZO]{\grl{δοξάζω}}\gr{,}

		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-14}

			If by any means I may provoke to emulation%
			\footnote{%
				% \label{}%
				\gr{παραζηλόω}, ``\textbf{provoke to jealousy, \textit{make jealous}}.''
				}
			them which are my flesh, and might save some of them.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἴ πως παραζηλώσω μου τὴν σάρκα καὶ σώσω τινὰς ἐξ αὐτῶν.}

		% ---

			For if the casting away of them be the reconciling of the world, what shall the receiving of them be, but life from the dead?

			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ γὰρ ἡ ἀποβολὴ αὐτῶν καταλλαγὴ κόσμου, τίς ἡ πρόσλημψις εἰ μὴ ζωὴ ἐκ νεκρῶν;}

		% ---

			For if the firstfruit be holy, the lump is also holy: and if the root be holy, so are the branches.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ δὲ ἡ ἀπαρχὴ ἁγία, καὶ τὸ φύραμα· καὶ εἰ ἡ ῥίζα ἁγία, καὶ οἱ κλάδοι.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:17} % *ROMANS-11-17 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-17}

			And if some of the branches be broken off, and thou, being a wild olive tree, wert graffed in among them, and with them partakest of the root and fatness of the olive tree;

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Εἰ δέ τινες τῶν κλάδων ἐξεκλάσθησαν, σὺ δὲ ἀγριέλαιος ὢν ἐνεκεντρίσθης ἐν αὐτοῖς καὶ συγκοινωνὸς τῆς ῥίζης τῆς πιότητος τῆς ἐλαίας ἐγένου,}

					% \item[SPNT]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:18} % *ROMANS-11-18 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-18}

			Boast not against the branches. But if thou boast, thou bearest not the root, but the root thee.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{μὴ κατακαυχῶ τῶν κλάδων· εἰ δὲ κατακαυχᾶσαι, οὐ σὺ τὴν ῥίζαν βαστάζεις ἀλλὰ ἡ ῥίζα σέ.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:19} % *ROMANS-11-19 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-19}

			Thou wilt say then, The branches were broken off, that I might be graffed in.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἐρεῖς οὖν· Ἐξεκλάσθησαν κλάδοι ἵνα ἐγὼ ἐγκεντρισθῶ.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:20} % *ROMANS-11-20 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-20}

			Well; because of unbelief they were broken off, and thou standest by faith. Be not highminded, but fear:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καλῶς· τῇ ἀπιστίᾳ ἐξεκλάσθησαν, σὺ δὲ τῇ πίστει ἕστηκας. μὴ ὑψηλὰ φρόνει, ἀλλὰ φοβοῦ·}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:21} % *ROMANS-11-21 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-21}

			For if God spared not the natural branches, take heed lest he also spare not thee.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ γὰρ ὁ θεὸς τῶν κατὰ φύσιν κλάδων οὐκ ἐφείσατο, οὐδὲ σοῦ φείσεται.}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:22} % *ROMANS-11-22 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-22}

			Behold therefore the goodness and severity of God: on them which fell, severity; but toward thee, goodness, if thou continue in his goodness: otherwise thou also shalt be cut off.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἴδε οὖν χρηστότητα καὶ ἀποτομίαν θεοῦ· ἐπὶ μὲν τοὺς πεσόντας ἀποτομία, ἐπὶ δὲ σὲ χρηστότης θεοῦ, ἐὰν ἐπιμένῃς τῇ χρηστότητι, ἐπεὶ καὶ σὺ ἐκκοπήσῃ.}

					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:23} % *ROMANS-11-23 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-23}

			And they also, if they abide not still in unbelief, shall be graffed in: for God is able to graff them in again.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{κἀκεῖνοι δέ, ἐὰν μὴ ἐπιμένωσι τῇ ἀπιστίᾳ, ἐγκεντρισθήσονται· δυνατὸς γάρ ἐστιν ὁ θεὸς πάλιν ἐγκεντρίσαι αὐτούς.}

					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:24} % *ROMANS-11-24 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-24}

			For if thou wert cut out of the olive tree which is wild by nature, and wert graffed contrary to nature into a good olive tree: how much more shall these, which be the natural branches, be graffed into their own olive tree?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{εἰ γὰρ σὺ ἐκ τῆς κατὰ φύσιν ἐξεκόπης ἀγριελαίου καὶ παρὰ φύσιν ἐνεκεντρίσθης εἰς καλλιέλαιον, πόσῳ μᾶλλον οὗτοι οἱ κατὰ φύσιν ἐγκεντρισθήσονται τῇ ἰδίᾳ ἐλαίᾳ.}

					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]

					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:25} % *ROMANS-11-25 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-25}

			For I would not, brethren, that ye should be ignorant of this mystery, lest ye should be wise in your own conceits; that blindness in part is happened to Israel, until the fulness of the Gentiles be come in.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Οὐ γὰρ θέλω ὑμᾶς ἀγνοεῖν, ἀδελφοί, τὸ μυστήριον τοῦτο, ἵνα μὴ ἦτε ἑαυτοῖς φρόνιμοι, ὅτι πώρωσις ἀπὸ μέρους τῷ Ἰσραὴλ γέγονεν ἄχρι οὗ τὸ πλήρωμα τῶν ἐθνῶν εἰσέλθῃ,}
					% \item[AB ]
					% \item[SPNT]
					% \item[NTR1]
					% \item[NTR2]
					% \item[NTX*]
					% \item[NTXX*]
					% \item[NTXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXX*]
					% \item[NTXXXXXX*]
				\end{compactdesc}
			\end{framed}

		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-26}

			And so all Israel shall be saved: as it is written, There shall come out of Sion the Deliverer, and shall turn away ungodliness from Jacob:

			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ οὕτως πᾶς Ἰσραὴλ σωθήσεται· καθὼς γέγραπται· Ἥξει ἐκ Σιὼν ὁ ῥυόμενος, ἀποστρέψει ἀσεβείας ἀπὸ Ἰακώβ.}

					% \item[SPNT]

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:27} % *ROMANS-11-27 
		% ---

			For this is my covenant unto them, when I shall take away their sins.

				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{καὶ αὕτη αὐτοῖς ἡ παρ᾽ ἐμοῦ διαθήκη, ὅταν ἀφέλωμαι τὰς ἁμαρτίας αὐτῶν.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:28} % *ROMANS-11-28 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-28}

			As concerning the gospel, they are enemies for your sakes: but as touching the election, they are beloved for the fathers' sakes.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{κατὰ μὲν τὸ εὐαγγέλιον ἐχθροὶ δι᾽ ὑμᾶς, κατὰ δὲ τὴν ἐκλογὴν ἀγαπητοὶ διὰ τοὺς πατέρας·}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:29} % *ROMANS-11-29 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-29}

			 For the gifts and calling of God are without repentance.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ἀμεταμέλητα γὰρ τὰ χαρίσματα καὶ ἡ κλῆσις τοῦ θεοῦ.}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:30} % *ROMANS-11-30 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-30}

			For as ye in times past have not believed God, yet have now obtained mercy through their unbelief:

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὥσπερ γὰρ ὑμεῖς ποτε ἠπειθήσατε τῷ θεῷ, νῦν δὲ ἠλεήθητε τῇ τούτων ἀπειθείᾳ,}

			\end{framed}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Romans 11:31} % *ROMANS-11-31 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-31}

			Even so have these also now not believed, that through your mercy they also may obtain mercy.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{οὕτως καὶ οὗτοι νῦν ἠπείθησαν τῷ ὑμετέρῳ ἐλέει ἵνα καὶ αὐτοὶ νῦν ἐλεηθῶσιν·}

		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-32}

			For God hath concluded them all in unbelief, that he might have mercy upon all.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{συνέκλεισεν γὰρ ὁ θεὸς τοὺς πάντας εἰς ἀπείθειαν ἵνα τοὺς πάντας ἐλεήσῃ.}

		\paragraph{Romans 11:33} % *ROMANS-11-33 
		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-33}

			O the depth of the riches both of the wisdom and knowledge of God! how unsearchable%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[JACOB-4-8]{Jacob 4:8}.
					}
			are his judgments, and his ways past finding out!

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Ὦ βάθος πλούτου καὶ σοφίας καὶ γνώσεως θεοῦ· ὡς ἀνεξεραύνητα τὰ κρίματα αὐτοῦ καὶ ἀνεξιχνίαστοι αἱ ὁδοὶ αὐτοῦ.}

		% ---

			For who hath known the mind of the Lord? or who hath been his counsellor?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{Τίς γὰρ ἔγνω νοῦν κυρίου; ἢ τίς σύμβουλος αὐτοῦ ἐγένετο;}

		\paragraph{Romans 11:35} % *ROMANS-11-35 
		% ---

			Or who hath first given to him, and it shall be recompensed unto him again?

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}

		% ---
			\label{ROMANS-11-36}

			For of him, and through him, and to him, are all things: to whom be glory for ever. Amen.

			\begin{framed}
			\scriptsize
				\begin{compactdesc}
					\item[SBLG] \gr{ὅτι ἐξ αὐτοῦ καὶ δι᾽ αὐτοῦ καὶ εἰς αὐτὸν τὰ πάντα· αὐτῷ ἡ δόξα εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας, ἀμήν.}
